<script id="mvp-custom-js-after">
	jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
	$(window).on("load", function(){
	var leaderHeight = $("#mvp-leader-wrap").outerHeight();
	var navHeight = $("#mvp-main-head-wrap").outerHeight();
	var headerHeight = navHeight + leaderHeight;
	var previousScroll = 0;
	$(window).scroll(function(event){
			var scroll = $(this).scrollTop();
			if ($(window).scrollTop() > headerHeight){
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").addClass("mvp-nav-small-fixed");
				$("#mvp-main-body-wrap").css("margin-top", navHeight );
			} else {
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").removeClass("mvp-nav-small-fixed");
				$("#mvp-main-body-wrap").css("margin-top","0");
			}
			if ($(window).scrollTop() > headerHeight + 50){
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").addClass("mvp-fixed");
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").addClass("mvp-fixed-shadow");
				$(".mvp-fly-top").addClass("mvp-to-top");
	    		if(scroll < previousScroll) {
					$("#mvp-main-nav-small").removeClass("mvp-soc-mob-up");
					$("#mvp-soc-mob-wrap").removeClass("mvp-soc-mob-up");
					$(".mvp-drop-nav-title").removeClass("mvp-nav-small-post");
					$(".mvp-nav-menu").show();
				} else {
					$("#mvp-main-nav-small").addClass("mvp-soc-mob-up");
					$("#mvp-soc-mob-wrap").addClass("mvp-soc-mob-up");
					$(".mvp-drop-nav-title").addClass("mvp-nav-small-post");
					$(".mvp-nav-menu").hide();
				}
			} else {
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").removeClass("mvp-fixed");
				$("#mvp-main-nav-small").removeClass("mvp-fixed-shadow");
	    		$(".mvp-fly-top").removeClass("mvp-to-top");
			}
			previousScroll = scroll;
	});
	});
	});
	

	jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
	$(".menu-item-has-children a").click(function(event){
	  event.stopPropagation();
	  
  	});

	$(".menu-item-has-children").click(function(){
    	  $(this).addClass("toggled");
    	  if($(".menu-item-has-children").hasClass("toggled"))
    	  {
    	  $(this).children("ul").toggle();
	  $(".mvp-fly-nav-menu").getNiceScroll().resize();
	  }
	  $(this).toggleClass("tog-minus");
    	  return false;
  	});

	// Main Menu Scroll
	$(window).on("load", function(){
	  $(".mvp-fly-nav-menu").niceScroll({cursorcolor:"#888",cursorwidth: 7,cursorborder: 0,zindex:999999});
	});
	});
	

	jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
	$(".infinite-content").infinitescroll({
	  navSelector: ".mvp-nav-links",
	  nextSelector: ".mvp-nav-links a:first",
	  itemSelector: ".infinite-post",
	  errorCallback: function(){ $(".mvp-inf-more-but").css("display", "none") }
	});
	$(window).unbind(".infscr");
	$(".mvp-inf-more-but").click(function(){
   		$(".infinite-content").infinitescroll("retrieve");
        	return false;
	});
	$(window).on("load", function(){
		if ($(".mvp-nav-links a").length) {
			$(".mvp-inf-more-but").css("display","inline-block");
		} else {
			$(".mvp-inf-more-but").css("display","none");
		}
	});
	});
	
jQuery(function($) {
	var ajaxUrl = "https:\/\/www.unite.ai\/wp-admin\/admin-ajax.php";

	function numericAttr($el, name, fallback) {
		var value = parseInt($el.attr(name), 10);
		return isNaN(value) ? fallback : value;
	}

	function markSeen($content) {
		var seen = {};
		$content.find('.infinite-post').each(function() {
			var $item = $(this);
			var key = $item.attr('data-ua-post-id') || $item.find('a[rel="bookmark"]').attr('href') || $item.find('a').first().attr('href');
			if (key) {
				seen[key] = true;
			}
		});
		return seen;
	}

	function findContent($button) {
		var $content = $button.closest('.mvp-main-blog-body').find('.infinite-content').first();
		return $content.length ? $content : $('.infinite-content').first();
	}

	function bindArchiveLoadMore() {
		var $buttons = $('a.mvp-inf-more-but[data-ua-load-more="1"]');

		$buttons.each(function() {
			var $button = $(this);
			var $content = findContent($button);
			$button.data('uaDefaultText', $.trim($button.text()) || 'More Posts');
			$button.data('uaSeen', markSeen($content));
		});

		$buttons.off('click').on('click.uaArchiveLoadMore', function(event) {
			event.preventDefault();
			event.stopImmediatePropagation();

			var $button = $(this);
			var $content = findContent($button);
			var page = numericAttr($button, 'data-ua-next-page', 2);
			var defaultText = $button.data('uaDefaultText') || 'More Posts';

			if ($button.data('uaLoading') || !$content.length) {
				return false;
			}

			$button.data('uaLoading', true).attr('aria-busy', 'true').text('Loading...');

			$.ajax({
				url: ajaxUrl,
				method: 'POST',
				dataType: 'json',
				data: {
					action: 'ua_archive_load_more',
					archive_type: $button.attr('data-ua-archive-type') || '',
					term_id: $button.attr('data-ua-term-id') || '',
					author_id: $button.attr('data-ua-author-id') || '',
					author_slug: $button.attr('data-ua-author-slug') || '',
					layout: $button.attr('data-ua-layout') || 'archive',
					page: page,
					per_page: numericAttr($button, 'data-ua-per-page', 15)
				}
			}).done(function(response) {
				if (!response || !response.success || !response.data || !response.data.html) {
					$button.hide();
					return;
				}

				var seen = $button.data('uaSeen') || {};
				var nodes = $.parseHTML($.trim(response.data.html), document, true) || [];
				var $items = $(nodes).filter('.infinite-post');
				var added = 0;

				if (!$items.length) {
					$items = $(nodes).find('.infinite-post');
				}

				$items.each(function() {
					var $item = $(this);
					var key = $item.attr('data-ua-post-id') || $item.find('a[rel="bookmark"]').attr('href') || $item.find('a').first().attr('href');

					if (key && seen[key]) {
						return;
					}

					if (key) {
						seen[key] = true;
					}

					$content.append($item);
					added++;
				});

				$button.data('uaSeen', seen);
				$button.attr('data-ua-next-page', response.data.next_page || page + 1);
				if (response.data.max_pages) {
					$button.attr('data-ua-max-pages', response.data.max_pages);
				}

				if (!response.data.has_more || added === 0) {
					$button.hide();
				} else {
					$button.text(defaultText).show();
				}
			}).fail(function() {
				$button.hide();
			}).always(function() {
				$button.data('uaLoading', false).removeAttr('aria-busy');
				if ($button.is(':visible') && $button.text() === 'Loading...') {
					$button.text(defaultText);
				}
			});

			return false;
		});
	}

	bindArchiveLoadMore();
	$(window).on('load', bindArchiveLoadMore);
});
//# sourceURL=mvp-custom-js-after
</script>

var UALangSwitcher = <script id="ua-left-rail-context-shared-js-after">
(function(){
  var data = {"lang":"zh-cn","labels":{"title":"\u8981\u70b9","articleStats":"\u6587\u7ae0\u7edf\u8ba1","readingTime":"\u9605\u8bfb\u65f6\u95f4","published":"\u53d1\u5e03\u65f6\u95f4","updated":"\u66f4\u65b0\u65f6\u95f4","sources":"\u6765\u6e90","wordCount":"\u5b57\u6570","onThisPage":"\u672c\u9875\u5185\u5bb9","securities":"\u8bc1\u5238","crypto":"\u52a0\u5bc6\u8d27\u5e01","min":"\u5206\u949f"},"why":"\u60a8\u662f\u5426\u66fe\u7d93\u8b80\u904e\u67d0\u4e9b\u5167\u5bb9\uff0c\u7acb\u5373\u5c31\u77e5\u9053\u5b83\u662f\u7531 AI \u64b0\u5beb\u7684\uff1f\u60a8\u53ef\u80fd\u6703\u6ce8\u610f\u5230\u67d0\u4e9b\u904e\u5ea6\u4f7f\u7528\u7684\u8a5e\u5f59\u3001\u91cd\u8907\u7684\u8a5e\u5f59\u6216\u77ed\u8a9e\uff0c\u6216\u8005\u904e\u5ea6\u4f7f\u7528\u67d0\u4e9b\u6d41\u884c\u8a5e\u5f59\u548c\u8853\u8a9e\uff08\u7b49\u7b49\uff09\u3002\u4f46\u662f\uff0c\u9019\u5c31\u662f\u70ba\u4ec0\u9ebc WriteHuman \u5b58\u5728\u7684\u539f\u56e0\u3002\u5b83\u89e3\u6c7a\u4e86\u4e00\u500b\u65e5\u76ca\u589e\u9577\u7684\u6311\u6230\uff1a\u5982\u4f55\u77ac\u9593 \u5275\u5efa\u807d\u8d77\u4f86\u50cf\u771f\u4eba\u5beb\u7684\u6587\u672c\u3002\u5728\u9019\u500b WriteHuman \u8a55\u6e2c\u4e2d\uff0c\u6211\u5c07\u8a0e\u8ad6\u5176\u512a\u7f3a\u9ede\u3001\u5b83\u662f\u4ec0\u9ebc\u3001\u8ab0\u662f\u5b83\u7684\u6700\u4f73\u4f7f\u7528\u8005\u3001\u4ee5\u53ca\u5176\u95dc\u9375\u529f\u80fd\u3002\u7136\u5f8c\uff0c\u6211\u5c07\u5c55\u793a\u5982\u4f55\u4f7f\u7528 WriteHuman \u5c07\u6211\u7684 AI \u751f\u6210\u7684\u6587\u672c\u77ac\u9593\u8f49\u63db\u70ba\u807d\u8d77\u4f86\u50cf\u4eba\u5beb\u7684\u5167\u5bb9\uff01\u6211\u5c07\u5728\u6bd4\u8f03 WriteHuman \u8207\u6211\u7684\u524d\u4e09\u500b\u66ff\u4ee3\u65b9\u6848\uff08Phrasly\u3001Undetectable AI \u548c Writesonic \u7684\u514d\u8cbb AI \u4eba\u6027\u5316\u5de5\u5177\uff09\u5f8c\u5b8c\u6210\u672c\u6587\u3002\u7121\u8ad6\u60a8\u662f\u535a\u5ba2\u4f5c\u8005\u3001\u5e02\u5834\u71df\u92b7\u4eba\u54e1\u9084\u662f\u5b78\u751f\uff0cWriteHuman \u90fd\u53ef\u4ee5\u5e6b\u52a9\u5c07 AI \u751f\u6210\u7684\u6587\u672c\u8f49\u63db\u70ba\u771f\u5be6\u3001\u5f15\u4eba\u5165\u52dd\u548c\u6613\u65bc\u7406\u89e3\u7684\u5167\u5bb9\u3002\u5b83\u4e0d\u50c5\u50c5\u662f\u907f\u514d AI\u2026","keyPoint1":"WriteHuman\u80fd\u5c06AI\u6587\u672c\u8f6c\u5316\u4e3a\u7c7b\u4eba\u5185\u5bb9\uff0c\u4f46\u5728\u5e94\u5bf9Turnitin\u7b49\u9ad8\u7ea7\u68c0\u6d4b\u5de5\u5177\u65f6\u4ecd\u663e\u4e0d\u8db3\u3002","keyPoint2":"\u8be5\u5de5\u5177\u63d0\u4f9b\u53ef\u81ea\u5b9a\u4e49\u7684\u8bed\u6c14\u9009\u9879\u548c\u7b80\u6d01\u754c\u9762\uff0c\u4fbf\u4e8e\u5feb\u901f\u4f18\u5316\u5185\u5bb9\u3002","keyPoint3":"\u5176\u514d\u8d39\u8bd5\u7528\u4ec5\u9650\u4e8e\u4e09\u6b21200\u8bcd\u7684\u8bf7\u6c42\uff0c\u56e0\u6b64\u6700\u9002\u5408\u5904\u7406\u5c0f\u578b\u4efb\u52a1\u3002","useBullets":1,"minutes":1,"words":192,"published":"1\u6708 23, 2025","updated":"5\u6708 19, 2026","showUpdated":1,"sourcesExternal":4,"securities":[],"crypto":[]};

  function isDesktop(){
    return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1100px)').matches;
  }

  function hasExistingLeftMenu(){
    if (document.body.classList.contains('has__cat_menu')) return true;
    if (document.querySelector('.category__sticky__menu')) return true;
    return false;
  }

  function uniqPush(arr, seen, key, obj){
    if (seen[key]) return;
    seen[key] = true;
    arr.push(obj);
  }

  function bulletHtml(items){
    var out = '<ul class="ua-rail__bullets">';
    items.forEach(function(item){
      out += '<li>' + escapeHtml(item) + '</li>';
    });
    out += '</ul>';
    return out;
  }

  function hydrateAssetsFromDOM(){
    var securities = Array.isArray(data.securities) ? data.securities.slice() : [];
    var crypto = Array.isArray(data.crypto) ? data.crypto.slice() : [];
    var seenS = {};
    var seenC = {};

    securities.forEach(function(it){
      if (it && it.e && it.s) seenS[String(it.e).toUpperCase() + '|' + String(it.s).toUpperCase()] = true;
    });
    crypto.forEach(function(it){
      if (it && it.e && it.s) seenC[String(it.e).toUpperCase() + '|' + String(it.s).toUpperCase()] = true;
    });

    var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('.sio-stock-price-wrapper[data-exchange][data-symbol]');
    nodes.forEach(function(n){
      var ex  = (n.getAttribute('data-exchange') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      var sym = (n.getAttribute('data-symbol') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!ex || !sym) return;

      var key = ex + '|' + sym;
      var path = ex === 'CRYPTO'
        ? ('/cryptocurrency/' + encodeURIComponent(sym) + '/')
        : ('/' + encodeURIComponent(ex.toLowerCase()) + '/' + encodeURIComponent(sym) + '/');
      var obj = { e: ex, s: sym, u: ('https://www.securities.io' + path) };

      if (ex === 'CRYPTO') {
        uniqPush(crypto, seenC, key, obj);
      } else {
        uniqPush(securities, seenS, key, obj);
      }
    });

    data.securities = securities.slice(0, 8);
    data.crypto = crypto.slice(0, 8);
  }

  function buildAssetSection(title, items){
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || !items.length) return '';

    var html = '<div class="ua-rail__assets">';
    html += '<div class="ua-rail__h">' + escapeHtml(title) + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="ua-rail__chips">';
    items.slice(0, 8).forEach(function(it){
      if (!it || !it.s || !it.u) return;
      html += '<a class="ua-rail__chip notranslate" translate="no" data-no-translate data-xf-no-translate href="' + escapeAttr(it.u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + escapeHtml(String(it.s)) + '</a>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  function buildAssetsBlock(){
    hydrateAssetsFromDOM();

    var securities = Array.isArray(data.securities) ? data.securities : [];
    var crypto = Array.isArray(data.crypto) ? data.crypto : [];

    if (!securities.length && !crypto.length) return '';

    var html = '';
    if (securities.length) {
      html += buildAssetSection(data.labels && data.labels.securities ? data.labels.securities : 'Securities', securities);
    }
    if (crypto.length) {
      html += buildAssetSection(data.labels && data.labels.crypto ? data.labels.crypto : 'Crypto', crypto);
    }

    return html;
  }

  function buildPanel(){
    var wrap = document.getElementById('mvp-side-wrap');
    if (!wrap) return;
    if (!isDesktop()) return;
    if (hasExistingLeftMenu()) return;
    if (wrap.querySelector('.ua-rail')) return;

    wrap.classList.add('ua-rail-mounted');

    var updatedRow = '';
    if (data.showUpdated) {
      updatedRow = '<div class="ua-rail__row"><span>' + escapeHtml(data.labels.updated) + '</span><b>' + escapeHtml(data.updated) + '</b></div>';
    }

    var sourcesRow = '';
    if (typeof data.sourcesExternal === 'number' && data.sourcesExternal > 0) {
      sourcesRow = '<div class="ua-rail__row"><span>' + escapeHtml(data.labels.sources) + '</span><b>' + escapeHtml(String(data.sourcesExternal)) + '</b></div>';
    }

    var bullets = [data.keyPoint1, data.keyPoint2, data.keyPoint3].filter(function(v){
      return String(v || '').trim() !== '';
    });

    var keyPointsHtml = '';
    if (data.useBullets && bullets.length === 3) {
      keyPointsHtml = bulletHtml(bullets);
    } else {
      keyPointsHtml = '<p class="ua-rail__why">' + escapeHtml(data.why) + '</p>';
    }

    var assetsBlock = buildAssetsBlock();
    var assetsDivider = assetsBlock ? '<hr class="ua-rail__divider" />' : '';

    wrap.innerHTML =
      '<aside class="ua-rail notranslate" translate="no" data-no-translate data-xf-no-translate aria-label="' + escapeHtml(data.labels.title) + '">' +
        '<div class="ua-rail__card">' +
          '<div class="ua-rail__kicker">' + escapeHtml(data.labels.title) + '</div>' +
          keyPointsHtml +
          (assetsBlock ? assetsBlock : '') +
          assetsDivider +
          '<div class="ua-rail__h">' + escapeHtml(data.labels.articleStats) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="ua-rail__stats">' +
            '<div class="ua-rail__row"><span>' + escapeHtml(data.labels.readingTime) + '</span><b>' + escapeHtml(String(data.minutes)) + ' ' + escapeHtml(data.labels.min) + '</b></div>' +
            updatedRow +
            '<div class="ua-rail__row"><span>' + escapeHtml(data.labels.published) + '</span><b>' + escapeHtml(data.published) + '</b></div>' +
            sourcesRow +
            '<div class="ua-rail__row"><span>' + escapeHtml(data.labels.wordCount) + '</span><b>' + escapeHtml(String(data.words)) + '</b></div>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div class="ua-rail__tocWrap" style="display:none;">' +
            '<hr class="ua-rail__divider" />' +
            '<div class="ua-rail__h">' + escapeHtml(data.labels.onThisPage) + '</div>' +
            '<ul class="ua-rail__toc" id="ua-rail-toc"></ul>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</aside>';

    buildTOC();
  }

  function norm(s){
    return String(s || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  }

  function isExcludedHeading(h){
    if (!h || !h.textContent) return true;
    var t = norm(h.textContent);

    if (t === 'pros and cons' || t === 'pros & cons' || t === 'pros / cons' || t === 'pros' || t === 'cons') return true;
    if (/^pros\s*(?:&|and|\/)?\s*cons$/.test(t)) return true;

    try{
      var inside = h.closest(
        '.show__pros_n_cons, .show-pros-n-cons, .pros__cons, .pros-cons, .proscons,' +
        '[class*="pros"][class*="cons"], [id*="pros"][id*="cons"]'
      );
      if (inside) return true;
    }catch(e){}

    return false;
  }

  function buildTOC(){
    var toc = document.getElementById('ua-rail-toc');
    if (!toc) return;

    var root = document.getElementById('mvp-content-main') || document.querySelector('article') || document;

    var headings = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('h2'))
      .filter(function(h){
        if (!h.textContent || !h.textContent.trim()) return false;
        return !isExcludedHeading(h);
      });

    if (headings.length < 2) return;

    var tocWrap = document.querySelector('.ua-rail__tocWrap');
    if (tocWrap) tocWrap.style.display = 'block';

    headings.forEach(function(h, idx){
      if (!h.id){
        h.id = 'ua-rail-' + idx + '-' + h.textContent.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\W+/g, '-');
      }
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = '#' + h.id;
      a.textContent = h.textContent.trim();
      li.appendChild(a);
      toc.appendChild(li);
    });

    var links = Array.prototype.slice.call(toc.querySelectorAll('a'));

    function clearActive(){
      links.forEach(function(a){
        a.classList.remove('is-active');
      });
    }

    function setActiveById(id){
      clearActive();
      if (!id) return;
      links.forEach(function(a){
        if (a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + id) {
          a.classList.add('is-active');
        }
      });
    }

    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;

    var visibleIds = [];
    var hasUserScrolled = false;

    function getFirstVisibleId(){
      return visibleIds.length ? visibleIds[0] : '';
    }

    function markUserScrolled(){
      if (window.scrollY > 40) {
        hasUserScrolled = true;
        window.removeEventListener('scroll', markUserScrolled);
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('scroll', markUserScrolled, { passive: true });

    var obs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries){
      entries.forEach(function(entry){
        var id = entry.target.id;
        if (!id) return;

        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          if (visibleIds.indexOf(id) === -1) {
            visibleIds.push(id);
          }
        } else {
          visibleIds = visibleIds.filter(function(v){
            return v !== id;
          });
        }
      });

      visibleIds.sort(function(a, b){
        var elA = document.getElementById(a);
        var elB = document.getElementById(b);
        if (!elA || !elB) return 0;
        return elA.getBoundingClientRect().top - elB.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      });

      if (!hasUserScrolled) {
        clearActive();
        return;
      }

      setActiveById(getFirstVisibleId());
    }, { rootMargin: '-25% 0px -65% 0px', threshold: 0.01 });

    headings.forEach(function(h){
      obs.observe(h);
    });
  }

  function escapeHtml(str){
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function escapeAttr(str){
    return escapeHtml(String(str));
  }

  var t;
  function onResize(){
    clearTimeout(t);
    t = setTimeout(function(){
      if (isDesktop()) buildPanel();
    }, 120);
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    buildPanel();
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
  });
})();
//# sourceURL=ua-left-rail-context-shared-js-after
</script>
<script id="ua-keypoints-mobile-placement-js-after">
(function(){
  var data = {"title":"\u8981\u70b9","bullets":["WriteHuman\u80fd\u5c06AI\u6587\u672c\u8f6c\u5316\u4e3a\u7c7b\u4eba\u5185\u5bb9\uff0c\u4f46\u5728\u5e94\u5bf9Turnitin\u7b49\u9ad8\u7ea7\u68c0\u6d4b\u5de5\u5177\u65f6\u4ecd\u663e\u4e0d\u8db3\u3002","\u8be5\u5de5\u5177\u63d0\u4f9b\u53ef\u81ea\u5b9a\u4e49\u7684\u8bed\u6c14\u9009\u9879\u548c\u7b80\u6d01\u754c\u9762\uff0c\u4fbf\u4e8e\u5feb\u901f\u4f18\u5316\u5185\u5bb9\u3002","\u5176\u514d\u8d39\u8bd5\u7528\u4ec5\u9650\u4e8e\u4e09\u6b21200\u8bcd\u7684\u8bf7\u6c42\uff0c\u56e0\u6b64\u6700\u9002\u5408\u5904\u7406\u5c0f\u578b\u4efb\u52a1\u3002"],"previewText":"WriteHuman\u80fd\u5c06AI\u6587\u672c\u8f6c\u5316\u4e3a\u7c7b\u4eba\u5185\u5bb9\uff0c\u4f46\u5728\u5e94\u5bf9Turnitin\u7b49\u9ad8\u7ea7\u68c0\u6d4b\u5de5\u5177\u65f6\u4ecd\u663e\u4e0d\u8db3\u3002","viewMore":"\u67e5\u770b\u66f4\u591a","viewLess":"\u6536\u8d77"};

  function isMobile(){
    return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1099px)').matches;
  }

  function escapeHtml(str){
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g,'&amp;')
      .replace(/</g,'&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g,'&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g,'&#039;');
  }

  function buildBlock(){
    if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.bullets) || !data.bullets.length) return null;

    var wrap = document.createElement('section');
    wrap.className = 'ua-kpm notranslate';
    wrap.setAttribute('translate', 'no');
    wrap.setAttribute('data-no-translate', '');
    wrap.setAttribute('data-xf-no-translate', '');
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-label', data.title || 'Key Points');

    var panelId = 'ua-kpm-panel-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
    var preview = data.previewText ? escapeHtml(data.previewText) : '';
    var viewMore = escapeHtml(data.viewMore || 'View more');
    var viewLess = escapeHtml(data.viewLess || 'View less');

    var listHtml = '<ul class="ua-kpm__list">';
    data.bullets.forEach(function(item){
      listHtml += '<li>' + escapeHtml(item) + '</li>';
    });
    listHtml += '</ul>';

    wrap.innerHTML =
      '<div class="ua-kpm__card">' +
        '<button class="ua-kpm__button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="' + panelId + '">' +
          '<div class="ua-kpm__top">' +
            '<span class="ua-kpm__head">' +
              '<span class="ua-kpm__title">' + escapeHtml(data.title || 'Key Points') + '</span>' +
            '</span>' +
            '<svg class="ua-kpm__chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">' +
              '<path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>' +
            '</svg>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<span class="ua-kpm__preview">' +
            '<span class="ua-kpm__previewRow">' +
              '<span class="ua-kpm__dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
              '<span>' +
                '<span class="ua-kpm__previewText">' + preview + '</span><br>' +
                '<span class="ua-kpm__more" data-more-text="' + viewMore + '" data-less-text="' + viewLess + '">' + viewMore + '</span>' +
              '</span>' +
            '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
        '</button>' +
        '<div class="ua-kpm__panel" id="' + panelId + '" hidden>' +
          listHtml +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';

    var button = wrap.querySelector('.ua-kpm__button');
    var panel  = wrap.querySelector('.ua-kpm__panel');
    var moreEl = wrap.querySelector('.ua-kpm__more');

    button.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var expanded = button.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
      var nextState = !expanded;

      button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', nextState ? 'true' : 'false');
      wrap.classList.toggle('is-open', nextState);
      panel.hidden = !nextState;

      if (moreEl) {
        moreEl.textContent = nextState
          ? (moreEl.getAttribute('data-less-text') || 'View less')
          : (moreEl.getAttribute('data-more-text') || 'View more');
      }
    });

    return wrap;
  }

   function insertBlock(){
    if (!isMobile()) return;
    if (document.querySelector('.ua-kpm')) return;

    var block = buildBlock();
    if (!block) return;

    var featImg = document.getElementById('mvp-post-feat-img');
    var contentWrap = document.getElementById('mvp-content-wrap');

    if (featImg && featImg.parentNode) {
      var insertionTarget = featImg;
      var next = featImg.nextElementSibling;

      while (next) {
        var isCaption =
          next.matches('.wp-caption-text, figcaption, .mvp-post-img-cap, .mvp-feat-caption, .post-caption, .featured-caption') ||
          next.querySelector('.wp-caption-text, figcaption, .mvp-post-img-cap, .mvp-feat-caption, .post-caption, .featured-caption');

        if (!isCaption) {
          break;
        }

        insertionTarget = next;
        next = next.nextElementSibling;
      }

      insertionTarget.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', block);
      return;
    }

    if (contentWrap && contentWrap.parentNode) {
      contentWrap.parentNode.insertBefore(block, contentWrap);
      return;
    }
  }

  var resizeTimer;
  function handleResize(){
    clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
    resizeTimer = setTimeout(function(){
      var existing = document.querySelector('.ua-kpm');
      if (!isMobile()) {
        if (existing) existing.remove();
        return;
      }
      if (!existing) insertBlock();
    }, 120);
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    insertBlock();
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize, { passive: true });
  });
})();
//# sourceURL=ua-keypoints-mobile-placement-js-after
</script>

	<script id="ua-menu-nonlink-controls-js">
	(function () {
		function directNonlinkControl(item) {
			var children = item ? item.children : [];
			for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
				if (children[i].matches && children[i].matches('button.ua-menu-nonlink-control')) {
					return children[i];
				}
			}

			return null;
		}

		function syncExpandedState(item) {
			var button = directNonlinkControl(item);
			if (!button) return;

			var expanded = item.classList.contains('tog-minus')
				|| item.classList.contains('mega-toggle-on');

			button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', expanded ? 'true' : 'false');
		}

		function isDesktopMegaMenu(button) {
			if (!button || !button.closest || !button.closest('.max-mega-menu')) {
				return false;
			}

			return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(min-width: 769px)').matches;
		}

		function closeSiblingMegaItems(item) {
			var parent = item ? item.parentElement : null;
			if (!parent || !parent.children) return;

			for (var i = 0; i < parent.children.length; i++) {
				var sibling = parent.children[i];
				if (sibling !== item && sibling.classList && sibling.classList.contains('mega-toggle-on')) {
					sibling.classList.remove('mega-toggle-on');
					syncExpandedState(sibling);
				}
			}
		}

		function closeOpenMegaItems(scope) {
			if (!scope || !scope.querySelectorAll) return;

			var openItems = scope.querySelectorAll('li.mega-toggle-on');
			for (var i = 0; i < openItems.length; i++) {
				openItems[i].classList.remove('mega-toggle-on');
				syncExpandedState(openItems[i]);
			}
		}

		document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
			var button = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('button.ua-menu-nonlink-control') : null;
			if (button && button.parentElement && button.parentElement.classList.contains('mega-menu-item-has-children')) {
				if (isDesktopMegaMenu(button)) {
					closeSiblingMegaItems(button.parentElement);
					button.parentElement.classList.toggle('mega-toggle-on');
					syncExpandedState(button.parentElement);
					return;
				}

				closeSiblingMegaItems(button.parentElement);
				button.parentElement.classList.toggle('mega-toggle-on');
				syncExpandedState(button.parentElement);
			}

			var item = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('li.menu-item-has-children, li.mega-menu-item-has-children') : null;
			if (!item) return;

			window.setTimeout(function () {
				syncExpandedState(item);
			}, 0);
		});

		document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
			if (event.target && event.target.closest && event.target.closest('.max-mega-menu')) {
				return;
			}

			closeOpenMegaItems(document);
		});

		window.addEventListener('pageshow', function () {
			closeOpenMegaItems(document);
		});
	})();
	</script>
		<script id="ua-accessibility-page-fixes-js">
	(function() {
		function isFocusable(element) {
			return /^(A|BUTTON|INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA|SUMMARY)$/.test(element.tagName || '');
		}

		function cleanOne(element) {
			if (!element || !element.getAttribute || !element.setAttribute) {
				return;
			}

			var value = parseInt(element.getAttribute('tabindex'), 10);

			if (value > 0) {
				element.setAttribute('tabindex', isFocusable(element) ? '0' : '-1');
			}
		}

		function clean(root) {
			root = root || document;

			if (root.nodeType === 1) {
				cleanOne(root);
			}

			if (!root.querySelectorAll) {
				return;
			}

			var nodes = root.querySelectorAll('[tabindex]');

			for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
				cleanOne(nodes[i]);
			}
		}

		clean(document);

		if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
			document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
				clean(document);
			});
		}

		window.setTimeout(function() {
			clean(document);
		}, 250);

		window.setTimeout(function() {
			clean(document);
		}, 1000);

		if ('MutationObserver' in window) {
			new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
				for (var i = 0; i < mutations.length; i++) {
					if (mutations[i].type === 'attributes') {
						cleanOne(mutations[i].target);
						continue;
					}

					for (var j = 0; j < mutations[i].addedNodes.length; j++) {
						if (mutations[i].addedNodes[j].nodeType === 1) {
							clean(mutations[i].addedNodes[j]);
						}
					}
				}
			}).observe(document.documentElement, {
				attributes: true,
				attributeFilter: ['tabindex'],
				childList: true,
				subtree: true
			});
		}
	}());
	</script>

    { key: 'aiImagePrompt', label: 'AI Image Prompt Generator', href: '/ai-image-prompt-generator/', icon: 'wand', localize: true },
    { key: 'exifRemover', label: 'EXIF Metadata Remover', href: '/tools/exif-metadata-remover/', icon: 'file', localize: true },
    { key: 'imageCompressor', label: 'Image Compressor', href: '/tools/image-compressor/', icon: 'compress', localize: true },
    { key: 'imageConversions', label: 'Image Conversions', href: '/tools/image-conversions/', icon: 'gallery', localize: true },
    { key: 'imageCropper', label: 'Image Cropper', href: '/tools/image-cropper/', icon: 'crop', localize: true },
    { key: 'imageResizer', label: 'Image Resizer', href: '/tools/image-resizer/', icon: 'resize', localize: true },
    { key: 'socialCropper', label: 'Social Media Image Cropper', href: '/tools/social-media-image-cropper/', icon: 'phone', localize: true },
    { key: 'socialPreview', label: 'Social Preview Image Checker', href: '/social-preview-image-checker/', icon: 'preview', localize: true },
    { key: 'allImageTools', label: 'All Free Image Tools', href: '/tools/image-tools/', icon: 'grid', localize: true }
  ];

  var WRITING_ITEMS = [
    { key: 'grammarChecker', label: 'Grammar Checker', href: '/grammar-checker/', icon: 'check', localize: true },
    { key: 'grammarFixer', label: 'Grammar Fixer', href: '/grammar-fixer/', icon: 'edit', localize: true },
    { key: 'paragraphRewriter', label: 'Paragraph Rewriter', href: '/paragraph-rewriter/', icon: 'switch', localize: true },
    { key: 'rewriteClarity', label: 'Rewrite Text for Clarity', href: '/rewrite-text-for-clarity/', icon: 'pen', localize: true },
    { key: 'sentenceRewriter', label: 'Sentence Rewriter', href: '/sentence-rewriter/', icon: 'refresh', localize: true },
    { key: 'summarizer', label: 'Summarizer', href: '/summarizer/', icon: 'document', localize: true },
    { key: 'translationLanguages', label: 'Translation Languages', href: '/translation/', icon: 'globe', localize: true },
    { key: 'allWritingTools', label: 'All Free Writing Tools', href: '/writing-tools/', icon: 'grid', localize: true }
  ];

  var SECTIONS = [
    { key: 'imageTools', fallback: 'Image Tools', items: IMAGE_ITEMS },
    { key: 'writingTranslation', fallback: 'Writing & Translation', items: WRITING_ITEMS }
  ];

  var UTILITY_PATHS = IMAGE_ITEMS.concat(WRITING_ITEMS).map(function (item) { return item.href; }).concat([
    '/translation/languages/',
    '/translation/',
    '/writing-tools/'
  ]);

  var LANGUAGE_PREFIX_RE = /^\/(?:ar|fr|es|de|ja|ko|pt|tr|ru|uk|zh-cn|zh-tw|da|no|cs|el|hi|it|id|vi|pl|nl|th|ro|sv|fi)\//i;
  var COMPACT_TOP_LABELS = {
    es: { ai: 'Herramientas IA', thought: 'Expertos IA' },
    pt: { thought: 'Especialistas IA' },
    tr: { ai: 'AI Ara\u00e7lar\u0131', thought: 'AI Uzmanlar\u0131' },
    ru: { certs: '\u0421\u0435\u0440\u0442\u0438\u0444\u0438\u043a\u0430\u0442\u044b', events: '\u0421\u043e\u0431\u044b\u0442\u0438\u044f', thought: '\u042d\u043a\u0441\u043f\u0435\u0440\u0442\u044b \u0418\u0418' },
    el: { thought: '\u0395\u03b9\u03b4\u03b9\u03ba\u03bf\u03af \u03a4\u039d' }
  };

  function topLevelItems(menu) {
    return Array.prototype.filter.call(menu.children || [], function (child) {
      return child && child.tagName === 'LI';
    });
  }

  function pathFromHref(href) {
    try {
      return new URL(href, window.location.origin).pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '/') || '/';
    } catch (error) {
      return '';
    }
  }

  function normalizedMenuPath(path) {
    return (path || '').replace(LANGUAGE_PREFIX_RE, '/').toLowerCase();
  }

  function isPath(link, path) {
    var currentPath = pathFromHref(link && link.getAttribute ? link.getAttribute('href') || '' : '');
    return !!link && (currentPath === path || normalizedMenuPath(currentPath) === String(path || '').toLowerCase());
  }

  function getLanguageKey() {
    var pathLang = (window.location.pathname.split('/')[1] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (pathLang.indexOf('zh') === 0) {
      return pathLang.indexOf('tw') !== -1 || pathLang.indexOf('hant') !== -1 ? 'zh-tw' : 'zh-cn';
    }
    if (TEXT[pathLang]) {
      return pathLang;
    }

    var htmlLang = (document.documentElement.getAttribute('lang') || '').toLowerCase().replace('_', '-');
    var raw = htmlLang || 'en';

    if (raw.indexOf('zh') === 0) {
      return raw.indexOf('tw') !== -1 || raw.indexOf('hant') !== -1 ? 'zh-tw' : 'zh-cn';
    }

    raw = raw.split('-')[0];
    return TEXT[raw] ? raw : 'en';
  }

  function getLanguagePrefix() {
    var first = (window.location.pathname.split('/')[1] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (!first || first === 'en') {
      return '';
    }
    return TEXT[first] ? first : '';
  }

  function textValue(key, fallback) {
    var lang = getLanguageKey();
    var langText = TEXT[lang] || TEXT.en;
    var toolText = TOOL_LABELS[lang] || TOOL_LABELS.en;

    if (key === 'imageTools') {
      return lang === 'en' ? 'Image Tools' : (langText.imageConversions || TEXT.en.imageConversions || fallback);
    }
    if (key === 'writingTranslation') {
      var writing = langText.writingTools || TEXT.en.writingTools || 'Writing';
      var translations = langText.translations || TEXT.en.translations || 'Translation';
      return lang === 'en' ? 'Writing & Translation' : writing + ' & ' + translations;
    }

    if (toolText && toolText[key]) {
      return toolText[key];
    }

    if (key === 'translationLanguages') {
      return langText.translations || fallback;
    }
    if (key === 'imageConversions') {
      return langText.imageConversions || fallback;
    }
    if (key === 'allWritingTools') {
      return lang === 'en' ? fallback : (langText.writingTools || fallback);
    }
    if (key === 'allImageTools') {
      return lang === 'en' ? fallback : (langText.imageConversions || fallback);
    }
    if (key === 'freeTools') {
      return langText.freeTools || TEXT.en.freeTools || fallback;
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function makeHref(item) {
    var path = item.href || '/';
    path = path.charAt(0) === '/' ? path : '/' + path;
    // Output buffers can localize menu data before this script runs. Normalize first
    // so the active language is applied exactly once, never /fr/fr/ or /fr/es/.
    path = path.replace(LANGUAGE_PREFIX_RE, '/');
    var prefix = getLanguagePrefix();
    if (prefix) {
      path = '/' + prefix + path;
    }
    return window.location.origin + path;
  }

  function findAiToolsTopItem(menu) {
    var items = topLevelItems(menu);
    var found = items.filter(function (item) {
      var sub = item.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu, :scope > ul.sub-menu');
      var text = (item.querySelector(':scope > a, :scope > button') || {}).textContent || '';
      return /AI Tools/i.test(text) || (sub && sub.querySelector('a[href*="/business-plan-generators/"]') && sub.querySelector('a[href*="/best-ai-tools-for-business/"]'));
    });
    return found[0] || null;
  }

  function findCertificationsTopItem(menu) {
    return topLevelItems(menu).filter(function (item) {
      return !!item.querySelector(':scope > ul a[href*="/blockchain-certifications/"]');
    })[0] || null;
  }

  function topLevelItemHasPath(item, path) {
    return Array.prototype.some.call(item.querySelectorAll(':scope a[href]'), function (link) {
      return normalizedMenuPath(pathFromHref(link.getAttribute('href') || '')) === path;
    });
  }

  function setTopLevelText(item, value) {
    var control = item && item.querySelector(':scope > a, :scope > button, :scope > span');
    if (!control || !value) {
      return;
    }

    var textNode = Array.prototype.filter.call(control.childNodes, function (node) {
      return node.nodeType === 3;
    })[0];

    if (textNode) {
      textNode.nodeValue = value;
    } else {
      control.insertBefore(document.createTextNode(value), control.firstChild);
    }
  }

  function applyCompactTopLabels(menu) {
    var labels = COMPACT_TOP_LABELS[getLanguageKey()];
    if (!labels) {
      return;
    }

    topLevelItems(menu).forEach(function (item) {
      if (labels.ai && (topLevelItemHasPath(item, '/best-ai-tools-for-business/') || topLevelItemHasPath(item, '/business-plan-generators/'))) {
        setTopLevelText(item, labels.ai);
      } else if (labels.certs && topLevelItemHasPath(item, '/blockchain-certifications/')) {
        setTopLevelText(item, labels.certs);
      } else if (labels.events && topLevelItemHasPath(item, '/conferences/')) {
        setTopLevelText(item, labels.events);
      } else if (labels.thought && topLevelItemHasPath(item, '/series/thought-leaders/')) {
        setTopLevelText(item, labels.thought);
      }
    });
  }

  function removeTopLevelFunding(menu) {
    topLevelItems(menu).forEach(function (item) {
      var link = item.querySelector(':scope > a, :scope > button');
      if (link && link.tagName === 'A' && isPath(link, '/series/funding/')) {
        item.parentNode.removeChild(item);
      }
    });
  }

  function renameBlockchain(root) {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/blockchain-certifications/"]'), function (link) {
      link.textContent = 'Blockchain';
    });
  }

  function removeUtilityLinks(root) {
    if (!root) {
      return;
    }

    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('a'), function (link) {
      var path = pathFromHref(link.getAttribute('href') || '');
      if (UTILITY_PATHS.indexOf(path) !== -1 || UTILITY_PATHS.indexOf(path.replace(/^\/[a-z]{2}(?:-[a-z]{2})?\//, '/')) !== -1) {
        var row = link.closest('li');
        if (row && row.parentNode) {
          row.parentNode.removeChild(row);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  function iconSvg(name) {
    var common = 'viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"';
    var paths = {
      wand: '<path d="M4 20l10-10"></path><path d="M13 5l1 3 3 1-3 1-1 3-1-3-3-1 3-1 1-3z"></path><path d="M5 4l.5 1.5L7 6l-1.5.5L5 8l-.5-1.5L3 6l1.5-.5L5 4z"></path>',
      image: '<rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2"></rect><path d="M7 15l3-3 2 2 3-4 2 5"></path><circle cx="8" cy="9" r="1.2"></circle>',
      scan: '<path d="M7 3H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v2"></path><path d="M17 3h2a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"></path><path d="M21 17v2a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-2"></path><path d="M7 21H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-2"></path><circle cx="12" cy="11" r="3"></circle><path d="M8 18c1-2 7-2 8 0"></path>',
      preview: '<rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2"></rect><path d="M7 14l3-3 2 2 2-3 3 4"></path><path d="M8 9h3"></path>',
      compress: '<path d="M8 3v5H3"></path><path d="M16 3v5h5"></path><path d="M8 21v-5H3"></path><path d="M16 21v-5h5"></path><path d="M8 8L3 3"></path><path d="M16 8l5-5"></path><path d="M8 16l-5 5"></path><path d="M16 16l5 5"></path>',
      resize: '<path d="M4 4h6"></path><path d="M4 4v6"></path><path d="M20 20h-6"></path><path d="M20 20v-6"></path><path d="M4 20l16-16"></path>',
      crop: '<path d="M6 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14"></path><path d="M2 6h14a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v14"></path>',
      phone: '<rect x="7" y="2" width="10" height="20" rx="2"></rect><path d="M11 18h2"></path>',
      file: '<path d="M7 3h7l4 4v14H7z"></path><path d="M14 3v5h5"></path><path d="M9 16h6"></path>',
      gallery: '<rect x="4" y="6" width="13" height="12" rx="2"></rect><path d="M8 6V4h12v12h-3"></path><path d="M7 15l3-3 2 2 2-3 2 4"></path>',
      check: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"></circle><path d="M8 12l3 3 5-6"></path>',
      edit: '<path d="M4 20l4-1 11-11-3-3L5 16z"></path><path d="M14 5l3 3"></path>',
      document: '<path d="M6 3h9l3 3v15H6z"></path><path d="M14 3v4h4"></path><path d="M9 12h6"></path><path d="M9 16h6"></path>',
      refresh: '<path d="M20 6v6h-6"></path><path d="M4 18v-6h6"></path><path d="M19 12a7 7 0 0 0-12-5"></path><path d="M5 12a7 7 0 0 0 12 5"></path>',
      switch: '<path d="M7 7h12l-3-3"></path><path d="M17 17H5l3 3"></path><path d="M19 7l-3 3"></path><path d="M5 17l3-3"></path>',
      pen: '<path d="M4 20l4-1 10-10-3-3L5 16z"></path><path d="M13 6l3 3"></path><path d="M5 20h14"></path>',
      globe: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"></circle><path d="M3 12h18"></path><path d="M12 3a14 14 0 0 1 0 18"></path><path d="M12 3a14 14 0 0 0 0 18"></path>',
      grid: '<rect x="4" y="4" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><rect x="14" y="4" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><rect x="4" y="14" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><rect x="14" y="14" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect>'
    };
    return '<svg ' + common + '>' + (paths[name] || paths.grid) + '</svg>';
  }

  function createToolLink(item, mobile) {
    var row = document.createElement('li');
    row.className = mobile ? 'menu-item menu-item-type-custom menu-item-object-custom ua-free-tools-mobile-row' : 'mega-menu-item mega-menu-item-type-custom mega-menu-item-object-custom ua-free-tools-row';

    var link = document.createElement('a');
    link.className = mobile ? 'ua-free-tools-mobile-link' : 'mega-menu-link ua-free-tools-link';
    link.href = makeHref(item);

    var icon = document.createElement('span');
    icon.className = 'ua-free-tools-icon ua-free-tools-icon-' + item.icon;
    icon.innerHTML = iconSvg(item.icon);
    link.appendChild(icon);

    var labelSpan = document.createElement('span');
    labelSpan.className = 'ua-free-tools-label';
    labelSpan.textContent = textValue(item.key, item.label);
    link.appendChild(labelSpan);

    var arrow = document.createElement('span');
    arrow.className = 'ua-free-tools-arrow';
    arrow.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    arrow.textContent = '\u203a';
    link.appendChild(arrow);

    row.appendChild(link);
    return row;
  }

  function freeToolsDesktopItem() {
    var parent = document.createElement('li');
    parent.id = 'mega-menu-item-ua-free-tools';
    parent.className = 'mega-menu-item mega-menu-item-type-custom mega-menu-item-object-custom mega-menu-item-has-children mega-align-bottom-left mega-menu-flyout ua-free-tools-menu';

    var button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'mega-menu-link ua-menu-nonlink-control';
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    button.setAttribute('aria-controls', 'mega-sub-menu-ua-free-tools');
    button.appendChild(document.createTextNode(textValue('freeTools', 'Free Tools')));

    var indicator = document.createElement('span');
    indicator.className = 'mega-indicator';
    indicator.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    button.appendChild(indicator);
    parent.appendChild(button);

    var submenu = document.createElement('ul');
    submenu.id = 'mega-sub-menu-ua-free-tools';
    submenu.className = 'mega-sub-menu';

    var panelItem = document.createElement('li');
    panelItem.className = 'mega-menu-item ua-free-tools-panel-item';
    var panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.className = 'ua-free-tools-panel';
    var grid = document.createElement('div');
    grid.className = 'ua-free-tools-grid';

    SECTIONS.forEach(function (section) {
      var sectionEl = document.createElement('section');
      sectionEl.className = 'ua-free-tools-section ua-free-tools-section-' + section.key;
      var heading = document.createElement('h3');
      heading.className = 'ua-free-tools-heading';
      heading.textContent = textValue(section.key, section.fallback);
      sectionEl.appendChild(heading);

      var list = document.createElement('ul');
      list.className = 'ua-free-tools-list';
      section.items.forEach(function (item) {
        list.appendChild(createToolLink(item, false));
      });
      sectionEl.appendChild(list);
      grid.appendChild(sectionEl);
    });

    panel.appendChild(grid);
    panelItem.appendChild(panel);
    submenu.appendChild(panelItem);
    parent.appendChild(submenu);
    return parent;
  }

  function positionDesktopPanel(parent) {
    var panel = parent && parent.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu');
    var wrap = document.querySelector('#mega-menu-wrap-main-menu') || parent;
    if (!panel || !wrap) {
      return;
    }
    var rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
    panel.style.setProperty('--ua-free-tools-top', Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.bottom)) + 'px');
  }

  function closeDesktop(parent) {
    var button = parent.querySelector(':scope > button.ua-menu-nonlink-control');
    parent.classList.remove('mega-toggle-on');
    if (button) {
      button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }
  }

  function bindDesktop(parent) {
    if (!parent || parent.getAttribute('data-ua-free-tools-bound') === '1') {
      return;
    }
    parent.setAttribute('data-ua-free-tools-bound', '1');

    var button = parent.querySelector(':scope > button.ua-menu-nonlink-control');
    if (!button) {
      return;
    }

    button.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      var shouldOpen = !parent.classList.contains('mega-toggle-on');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(parent.parentNode.querySelectorAll(':scope > li.mega-toggle-on'), closeDesktop);
      parent.classList.toggle('mega-toggle-on', shouldOpen);
      button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', shouldOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
      if (shouldOpen) {
        positionDesktopPanel(parent);
      }
    });

    parent.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
      positionDesktopPanel(parent);
    });

    button.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
        event.preventDefault();
        button.click();
      }
      if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        event.preventDefault();
        parent.classList.add('mega-toggle-on');
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        positionDesktopPanel(parent);
        var firstLink = parent.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu a');
        if (firstLink) {
          firstLink.focus();
        }
      }
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        closeDesktop(parent);
        button.focus();
      }
    });

    parent.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
      closeDesktop(parent);
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (!parent.contains(event.target)) {
        closeDesktop(parent);
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        closeDesktop(parent);
      }
    });
  }

  function buildDesktopFreeTools(menu) {
    if (!menu) {
      return;
    }

    removeTopLevelFunding(menu);
    renameBlockchain(menu);
    removeUtilityLinks(findAiToolsTopItem(menu));
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(menu.querySelectorAll(':scope > li.ua-free-tools-menu'), function (item) {
      item.parentNode.removeChild(item);
    });

    var aiTools = findAiToolsTopItem(menu);
    if (!aiTools) {
      return;
    }

    var freeTools = freeToolsDesktopItem();
    aiTools.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', freeTools);
    bindDesktop(freeTools);
    positionDesktopPanel(freeTools);
  }

  function freeToolsMobileItem() {
    var parent = document.createElement('li');
    parent.id = 'menu-item-ua-free-tools';
    parent.className = 'menu-item menu-item-type-custom menu-item-object-custom menu-item-has-children ua-free-tools-mobile-menu';

    var button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'ua-menu-nonlink-control';
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    button.textContent = textValue('freeTools', 'Free Tools');
    parent.appendChild(button);

    var submenu = document.createElement('ul');
    submenu.className = 'sub-menu ua-free-tools-mobile-submenu';

    [
      { key: 'imageConversions', label: 'Image Conversions', href: '/tools/image-conversions/' },
      { key: 'translationLanguages', label: 'Translations', href: '/translation/', localize: true },
      { key: 'allWritingTools', label: 'Writing Tools', href: '/writing-tools/', localize: true }
    ].forEach(function (item) {
      var row = document.createElement('li');
      row.className = 'menu-item menu-item-type-custom menu-item-object-custom ua-free-tools-mobile-row';

      var link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = makeHref(item);
      link.textContent = textValue(item.key, item.label);
      row.appendChild(link);

      submenu.appendChild(row);
    });

    parent.appendChild(submenu);

    function toggleMobileMenu(event) {
      if (event && event.target && event.target.closest('.ua-free-tools-mobile-submenu')) {
        return;
      }
      if (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (event.stopImmediatePropagation) {
          event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        }
      }

      var open = !parent.classList.contains('ua-free-tools-mobile-open');
      parent.classList.toggle('ua-free-tools-mobile-open', open);
      parent.classList.toggle('toggled', open);
      parent.classList.toggle('tog-minus', open);
      button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
    }

    parent.addEventListener('click', toggleMobileMenu);
    button.addEventListener('click', toggleMobileMenu);

    return parent;
  }

  function buildMobileFreeTools() {
    var menu = document.querySelector('#mvp-fly-menu-wrap .mvp-fly-nav-menu ul.menu');
    if (!menu) {
      return;
    }

    removeTopLevelFunding(menu);
    renameBlockchain(menu);
    removeUtilityLinks(menu);
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(menu.querySelectorAll(':scope > li.ua-free-tools-mobile-menu'), function (item) {
      item.parentNode.removeChild(item);
    });

    var aiTools = findAiToolsTopItem(menu);
    var certifications = findCertificationsTopItem(menu);
    var freeTools = freeToolsMobileItem();
    if (aiTools) {
      aiTools.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', freeTools);
    } else if (certifications) {
      certifications.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', freeTools);
    } else {
      menu.insertBefore(freeTools, menu.firstChild);
    }
  }

  function applyFreeToolsNavigation() {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#mega-menu-main-menu'), buildDesktopFreeTools);
    buildMobileFreeTools();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyFreeToolsNavigation, { once: true });
  } else {
    applyFreeToolsNavigation();
  }
  window.addEventListener('load', applyFreeToolsNavigation, { once: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#mega-menu-main-menu > li.ua-free-tools-menu'), positionDesktopPanel);
  });
  window.setTimeout(applyFreeToolsNavigation, 600);
})();
</script>
        <script id="uniteai-ai-tools-desktop-mega-menu">
(function () {
  if (window.__uniteAiToolsMegaMenu) return;

  function langKey() {
    var pathLang = (location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (TEXT[pathLang]) return pathLang;
    if (pathLang === 'zh-tw' && TEXT['zh-cn']) return 'zh-cn';
    var htmlLang = (document.documentElement.getAttribute('lang') || 'en').toLowerCase().replace('_', '-');
    if (TEXT[htmlLang]) return htmlLang;
    if (htmlLang.indexOf('zh') === 0) return 'zh-cn';
    var shortLang = htmlLang.split('-')[0];
    return TEXT[shortLang] ? shortLang : 'en';
  }

  function localePrefix(lang) {
    var firstPath = (location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || '').toLowerCase();
    return firstPath && (TEXT[firstPath] || (firstPath === 'zh-tw' && TEXT['zh-cn'])) && firstPath !== 'en' ? '/' + firstPath : '';
  }

  function t(lang, key) {
    return (TEXT[lang] && TEXT[lang][key]) || TEXT.en[key] || key;
  }

  function slugFromHref(href) {
    try {
      var parts = new URL(href, location.origin).pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
      return (parts[parts.length - 1] || '').toLowerCase();
    } catch (error) {
      return '';
    }
  }

  function cleanLabel(label) {
    return String(label || '')
      .replace(/\s*[\(（]?\s*(premium|free|gratis|gratuit|kostenlos|무료|livre|gratuito|ücretsiz|бесплатно|ฟรี)\s*[\)）]?\s*/ig, '')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }

  function itemLabel(lang, key, fallback, english) {
    if (TEXT[lang] && TEXT[lang][key]) return TEXT[lang][key];
    if (TEXT.en[key] && lang === 'en') return TEXT.en[key];
    var cleaned = cleanLabel(fallback);
    return cleaned || english;
  }

  function findAiToolsMenu() {
    var topItems = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#mega-menu-main-menu > li.mega-menu-item-has-children'));
    return topItems.find(function (item) {
      var slugs = Array.prototype.slice.call(item.querySelectorAll(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu a.mega-menu-link')).map(function (link) {
        return slugFromHref(link.href);
      });
      return slugs.indexOf('business-plan-generators') !== -1 &&
        slugs.indexOf('best-ai-writing-tools') !== -1 &&
        slugs.indexOf('best-ai-tools-for-business') !== -1 &&
        slugs.indexOf('blockchain-certifications') === -1 &&
        slugs.indexOf('conferences') === -1;
    });
  }

  function positionPanel(parent, panel) {
    if (!parent || !panel || window.innerWidth < 900) return;
    var width = Math.min(1480, Math.max(980, window.innerWidth - 72));
    var desiredLeft = Math.max(36, (window.innerWidth - width) / 2);
    if (desiredLeft + width > window.innerWidth - 36) desiredLeft = Math.max(36, window.innerWidth - width - 36);
    var parentLeft = parent.getBoundingClientRect().left;
    panel.style.width = width + 'px';
    panel.style.left = Math.round(desiredLeft - parentLeft) + 'px';
    panel.style.transform = 'none';
  }

  function buildAiToolsMegaMenu() {
    var parent = findAiToolsMenu();
    if (!parent) return;
    var subMenu = parent.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu');
    if (!subMenu) return;

    if (parent.getAttribute('data-ua-ai-tools-mega-ready') === '1') {
      positionPanel(parent, subMenu);
      return;
    }

    var lang = langKey();
    var prefix = localePrefix(lang);
    var linkMap = {};
    Array.prototype.slice.call(subMenu.querySelectorAll('a.mega-menu-link')).forEach(function (link) {
      var slug = slugFromHref(link.href);
      if (!slug) return;
      if (!linkMap[slug]) {
        linkMap[slug] = { href: link.href, text: cleanLabel(link.textContent), target: link.getAttribute('target'), rel: link.getAttribute('rel') };
      }
    });

    parent.classList.add('ua-ai-tools-mega-menu');
    parent.setAttribute('data-ua-ai-tools-mega-ready', '1');
    subMenu.setAttribute('dir', lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
    subMenu.innerHTML = '';

    var shell = document.createElement('li');
    shell.className = 'mega-menu-item ua-ai-tools-mega-shell';
    var grid = document.createElement('div');
    grid.className = 'ua-ai-tools-mega-grid';

    COLUMNS.forEach(function (column) {
      var section = document.createElement('section');
      section.className = 'ua-ai-tools-mega-column';
      var heading = document.createElement('h3');
      heading.className = 'ua-ai-tools-mega-heading';
      heading.textContent = t(lang, column.heading);
      section.appendChild(heading);

      var list = document.createElement('ul');
      list.className = 'ua-ai-tools-mega-list';
      column.items.forEach(function (item) {
        var slug = item[0];
        var icon = item[1];
        var key = item[2];
        var english = item[3];
        var existing = linkMap[slug] || {};

        var row = document.createElement('li');
        row.className = 'mega-menu-item ua-ai-tools-mega-row';
        row.setAttribute('data-ua-ai-icon', icon);

        var anchor = document.createElement('a');
        anchor.className = 'mega-menu-link';
        anchor.href = existing.href || (location.origin + prefix + '/' + slug + '/');
        if (existing.target) anchor.setAttribute('target', existing.target);
        if (existing.rel) anchor.setAttribute('rel', existing.rel);

        var label = document.createElement('span');
        label.className = 'ua-ai-tools-label';
        label.textContent = itemLabel(lang, key, existing.text, english);
        anchor.appendChild(label);
        row.appendChild(anchor);
        list.appendChild(row);
      });

      section.appendChild(list);
      grid.appendChild(section);
    });

    shell.appendChild(grid);
    subMenu.appendChild(shell);
    positionPanel(parent, subMenu);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', buildAiToolsMegaMenu, { once: true });
  } else {
    buildAiToolsMegaMenu();
  }
  window.addEventListener('load', buildAiToolsMegaMenu, { once: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
    var parent = document.querySelector('#mega-menu-main-menu > li.ua-ai-tools-mega-menu');
    var panel = parent && parent.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu');
    positionPanel(parent, panel);
  });
  window.setTimeout(buildAiToolsMegaMenu, 600);
})();
</script>

  ];

  function languageKey() {
    var firstPath = (location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (TEXT[firstPath]) return firstPath;
    if (firstPath === 'zh-tw' && TEXT['zh-cn']) return 'zh-cn';
    var htmlLang = (document.documentElement.getAttribute('lang') || 'en').toLowerCase().replace('_', '-');
    if (TEXT[htmlLang]) return htmlLang;
    if (htmlLang.indexOf('zh') === 0 && TEXT['zh-cn']) return 'zh-cn';
    var shortLang = htmlLang.split('-')[0];
    return TEXT[shortLang] ? shortLang : 'en';
  }

  function localizedText(lang, key) {
    var index = KEYS.indexOf(key);
    var pack = TEXT[lang] || TEXT.en;
    return index >= 0 && pack[index] ? pack[index] : TEXT.en[index];
  }

  function localePrefix(lang) {
    var firstPath = (location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || '').toLowerCase();
    return firstPath && (TEXT[firstPath] || (firstPath === 'zh-tw' && TEXT['zh-cn'])) && firstPath !== 'en' ? '/' + firstPath : '';
  }

  function slugFromHref(href) {
    try {
      var match = new URL(href, location.origin).pathname.match(/\/series\/([^\/]+)\/?/i);
      return match ? match[1].toLowerCase() : '';
    } catch (error) {
      return '';
    }
  }

  function findNewsMenu() {
    var topItems = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#mega-menu-main-menu > li.mega-menu-item-has-children'));
    return topItems.find(function (item) {
      var links = Array.prototype.slice.call(item.querySelectorAll(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu a.mega-menu-link[href*="/series/"]'));
      var slugs = links.map(function (link) { return slugFromHref(link.href); });
      var hasNewsCore = slugs.indexOf('artificial-intelligence') !== -1 && slugs.indexOf('acquisitions') !== -1 && slugs.indexOf('ethics') !== -1;
      var isOtherMenu = slugs.indexOf('blockchain-certifications') !== -1 || slugs.indexOf('conferences') !== -1;
      return hasNewsCore && !isOtherMenu;
    });
  }

  function buildNewsMegaMenu() {
    var parent = findNewsMenu();
    if (!parent || parent.getAttribute('data-ua-news-mega-ready') === '1') return;

    var subMenu = parent.querySelector(':scope > ul.mega-sub-menu');
    if (!subMenu) return;

    var lang = languageKey();
    var prefix = localePrefix(lang);
    var linkMap = {};
    Array.prototype.slice.call(subMenu.querySelectorAll('a.mega-menu-link[href*="/series/"]')).forEach(function (link) {
      var slug = slugFromHref(link.href);
      if (!slug || linkMap[slug]) return;
      linkMap[slug] = {
        href: link.href,
        target: link.getAttribute('target'),
        rel: link.getAttribute('rel'),
        current: /mega-current-menu-item|mega-current-menu-ancestor/.test((link.closest('li') || {}).className || '')
      };
    });

    parent.classList.add('ua-news-mega-menu');
    parent.setAttribute('data-ua-news-mega-ready', '1');
    subMenu.setAttribute('dir', lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
    subMenu.innerHTML = '';

    var shell = document.createElement('li');
    shell.className = 'mega-menu-item ua-news-mega-shell';
    var grid = document.createElement('div');
    grid.className = 'ua-news-mega-grid';

    COLUMNS.forEach(function (column) {
      var col = document.createElement('section');
      col.className = 'ua-news-mega-column';
      var heading = document.createElement('h3');
      heading.className = 'ua-news-mega-heading';
      heading.textContent = localizedText(lang, column.heading);
      col.appendChild(heading);

      var list = document.createElement('ul');
      list.className = 'ua-news-mega-list';
      column.items.forEach(function (item) {
        var slug = item[0];
        var icon = item[1];
        var labelKey = item[2];
        var existing = linkMap[slug] || {};
        var row = document.createElement('li');
        row.className = 'mega-menu-item ua-news-mega-row';
        row.setAttribute('data-ua-news-icon', icon);
        if (existing.current) row.classList.add('mega-current-menu-item');

        var anchor = document.createElement('a');
        anchor.className = 'mega-menu-link';
        anchor.href = existing.href || (location.origin + prefix + '/series/' + slug + '/');
        if (existing.target) anchor.setAttribute('target', existing.target);
        if (existing.rel) anchor.setAttribute('rel', existing.rel);

        var label = document.createElement('span');
        label.className = 'ua-news-label';
        label.textContent = localizedText(lang, labelKey);
        anchor.appendChild(label);
        row.appendChild(anchor);
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
      col.appendChild(list);
      grid.appendChild(col);
    });

    var footer = document.createElement('div');
    footer.className = 'ua-news-mega-footer';
    var footerCopy = document.createElement('div');
    footerCopy.className = 'ua-news-footer-copy';
    var footerTitle = document.createElement('span');
    footerTitle.className = 'ua-news-footer-title';
    footerTitle.textContent = localizedText(lang, 'footerExplore');
    var footerSub = document.createElement('span');
    footerSub.className = 'ua-news-footer-subtext';
    footerSub.textContent = localizedText(lang, 'footerSub');
    footerCopy.appendChild(footerTitle);
    footerCopy.appendChild(footerSub);

    var footerButton = document.createElement('a');
    footerButton.className = 'ua-news-footer-button';
    footerButton.href = location.origin + prefix + '/';
    footerButton.textContent = localizedText(lang, 'footerView');
    footer.appendChild(footerCopy);
    footer.appendChild(footerButton);

    shell.appendChild(grid);
    shell.appendChild(footer);
    subMenu.appendChild(shell);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', buildNewsMegaMenu, { once: true });
  } else {
    buildNewsMegaMenu();
  }
  window.addEventListener('load', buildNewsMegaMenu, { once: true });
  window.setTimeout(buildNewsMegaMenu, 600);
})();
</script>

                language: config.language,

                device: deviceType(),

                experiment_key: config.experimentKey || '',
                variant: config.variant || ''

                credentials: 'same-origin',
                keepalive: true,
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},

        }

        ['scroll', 'pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart'].forEach(function (name) {
            addEventListener(name, recordActivity, {passive: true});
        });

        function updateScroll() {
            var article = document.querySelector('.mvp-post-content, .mvp-post-main-in, article, main');
            var rect = article && article.getBoundingClientRect ? article.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
            var top = rect ? rect.top + scrollY : 0;
            var height = rect ? Math.max(1, rect.height) :
                Math.max(document.body.scrollHeight, document.documentElement.scrollHeight);
            var viewed = Math.max(0, Math.min(height, scrollY + innerHeight - top));
            maxScroll = Math.max(maxScroll, Math.min(100, Math.round(viewed / height * 100)));
        }
        addEventListener('scroll', updateScroll, {passive: true});
        addEventListener('resize', updateScroll, {passive: true});
        updateScroll();

        addEventListener('click', function (event) {
            var link = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('a[href]') : null;
            if (!link) return;
            var target;
            try { target = new URL(link.href, location.href); } catch (e) { return; }
            var kind = '';
            if (/\/goto\//i.test(target.pathname)) kind = 'partner';
            else if (link.closest('table')) kind = 'comparison';
            else if (/(^|\s)(cta|button|btn)(\s|$)/i.test(link.className || '')) kind = 'cta';
            if (!kind) return;
            var identifier = kind === 'partner' ?
                target.pathname.replace(/^.*\/goto\//i, '').replace(/\/+$/, '').split('/')[0] :
                (target.hash ? target.hash.replace(/^#/, '') : target.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop() || kind);
            send(payload('click', 0, false, {
                url_path: target.pathname,
                experiment_key: 'click_' + kind,
                variant: String(identifier || kind).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, '-').slice(0, 80)

            if (!document.hidden && Date.now() - lastActivity <= 30000) engaged += 5;
        }, 5000);

        function flushEngagement(isClosed, useBeacon) {

            var delta = Math.max(0, engaged - sentEngaged);

        send(payload('pageview', 0), false);
        if (config.experimentKey && config.variant) send(payload('experiment', 0), false);
    })({"endpoint":"https://www.unite.ai/wp-json/god-view/v1/event","token":"e20d145d8b","postId":296203,"originalId":211406,"language":"zh-cn","heartbeatMs":30000,"experimentKey":"","variant":""});
    </script>
    		<script>
		(function() {
			var enabledLanguages = ["en","ar","fr","es","de","ja","ko","pt","tr","ru","uk","zh-cn","da","no","cs","el","hi","it","id","vi","pl","nl","th","ro","sv","fi"];

			function cleanSearchLang() {
				var path = window.location && window.location.pathname ? window.location.pathname : '/';
				var parts = path.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '').split('/');
				var first = (parts[0] || '').toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, '-');

				if (first && first !== 'en' && enabledLanguages.indexOf(first) !== -1) {
					return first;
				}

				return 'original';
			}

			function cleanSearchAction(lang) {
				return lang && lang !== 'original' ? '/' + lang + '/' : '/';
			}

			function ensureHidden(form, name, value) {
				var input = form.querySelector('input[name="' + name + '"]');
				if (!input) {
					input = document.createElement('input');
					input.type = 'hidden';
					input.name = name;
					form.appendChild(input);
				}
				input.value = value;
			}

			function normalizeSearchForm(form) {
				if (!form || !form.querySelector) return false;

				var input = form.querySelector('input[name="s"]');
				if (!input) return false;

				var lang = cleanSearchLang();
				form.setAttribute('method', 'post');
				form.setAttribute('action', cleanSearchAction(lang));
				ensureHidden(form, '_ua_clean_search', '1');
				ensureHidden(form, 'ua_search_lang', lang);

				return true;
			}

			function normalizeSearchForms(scope) {
				var root = scope && scope.querySelectorAll ? scope : document;
				var forms = root.querySelectorAll('form#searchform, form.search-form, form');
				for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i++) {
					normalizeSearchForm(forms[i]);
				}
			}

			function normalizeSoon() {
				window.setTimeout(function() { normalizeSearchForms(document); }, 0);
			}

			document.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
				normalizeSearchForm(event.target);
			}, true);

			document.addEventListener('click', normalizeSoon, true);
			document.addEventListener('focusin', normalizeSoon, true);

			if (window.MutationObserver) {
				new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
					for (var i = 0; i < mutations.length; i++) {
						for (var j = 0; j < mutations[i].addedNodes.length; j++) {
							var node = mutations[i].addedNodes[j];
							if (node && node.nodeType === 1) {
								if (node.matches && node.matches('form')) {
									normalizeSearchForm(node);
								}
								normalizeSearchForms(node);
							}
						}
					}
				}).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
			}

			if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
				document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { normalizeSearchForms(document); });
			} else {
				normalizeSearchForms(document);
			}
		})();
		</script>

	@media (max-width: 767px) {

	<script>
	(function () {
		var thresholdHide = 180;
		var thresholdShow = 60;

		function updateLangVisibility() {
			if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
				document.body.classList.remove('mc-hide-mobile-lang');
				return;
			}

			if (window.scrollY > thresholdHide) {
				document.body.classList.add('mc-hide-mobile-lang');
			} else if (window.scrollY < thresholdShow) {
				document.body.classList.remove('mc-hide-mobile-lang');
			}
		}

		window.addEventListener('scroll', updateLangVisibility, { passive: true });
		window.addEventListener('resize', updateLangVisibility);
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', updateLangVisibility);
		updateLangVisibility();
	})();
	</script>
		<script id="ua-preferred-source-link-analytics">
	(function () {
		'use strict';
		document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
			var target = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('.preferred-source-link') : null;
			if (!target || !Array.isArray(window.dataLayer)) {
				return;
			}
			var deviceClass = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)').matches
				? 'mobile'
				: (window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1023px)').matches ? 'tablet' : 'desktop');
			var isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains('ua-nightmode') || document.body.classList.contains('ua-nightmode');
			window.dataLayer.push({
				event: 'preferred_source_click',
				site_name: target.getAttribute('data-site-name') || '',
				article_url: target.getAttribute('data-article-url') || window.location.href,
				article_id: target.getAttribute('data-article-id') || '',
				device_class: deviceClass,
				theme: isDark ? 'dark' : 'light'
			});
		}, false);
	}());
	</script>
	    <script>
    (function () {
        window.addEventListener('load', function () {
            try {
                var wraps = document.querySelectorAll('.mega-menu-wrap, .mvp-nav-wrap, #mvp-main-nav');
                wraps.forEach(function (el) {
                    if (el) {
                        el.style.visibility = 'visible';
                        el.style.opacity = '1';
                        if (el.style.display === 'none') el.style.display = '';
                    }
                });

                // Nudge layout in case the plugin missed initial measurement
                window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
            } catch (e) {}
        });
    })();

    <script id="unite-nightmode-js">(function () {
  var root = document.documentElement;

  var mobileQuery = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)') : { matches: false, addEventListener: function () {} };
  var darkQuery = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)') : { matches: false, addEventListener: function () {} };
  var VALID = { system: true, light: true, dark: true };
  var LABELS = {
    en: { appearance: 'Appearance', system: 'System', light: 'Light', dark: 'Dark' },
    fr: { appearance: 'Apparence', system: 'Systeme', light: 'Clair', dark: 'Sombre' },
    es: { appearance: 'Apariencia', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Claro', dark: 'Oscuro' },
    de: { appearance: 'Darstellung', system: 'System', light: 'Hell', dark: 'Dunkel' },
    it: { appearance: 'Aspetto', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Chiaro', dark: 'Scuro' },
    pt: { appearance: 'Aparencia', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Claro', dark: 'Escuro' }
  };

  function normalize(value) { return VALID[value] ? value : 'system'; }
  function getPreference() { try { return normalize(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)); } catch (e) { return 'system'; } }
  function savePreference(value) { try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, normalize(value)); } catch (e) {} }
  function resolvePreference(value) {
    var preference = normalize(value);
    if (preference === 'dark') return true;
    if (preference === 'light') return false;

  }
  function getSunIcon() {
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"></circle><path d="M12 2v2"></path><path d="M12 20v2"></path><path d="m4.93 4.93 1.41 1.41"></path><path d="m17.66 17.66 1.41 1.41"></path><path d="M2 12h2"></path><path d="M20 12h2"></path><path d="m6.34 17.66-1.41 1.41"></path><path d="m19.07 4.93-1.41 1.41"></path></svg>';
  }
  function getSystemIcon() {
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="12" rx="2"></rect><path d="M8 20h8"></path><path d="M12 16v4"></path></svg>';
  }
  function getLabels() {
    var lang = (root.lang || document.body.getAttribute('lang') || 'en').slice(0, 2).toLowerCase();
    return LABELS[lang] || LABELS.en;

  function updateControls(preference, enabled) {
    var normalized = normalize(preference);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ua-nightmode-toggle]').forEach(function (button) {

      button.setAttribute('aria-label', enabled ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode');
      if (label) label.textContent = enabled ? 'Light' : 'Night';
      if (icon) icon.innerHTML = enabled ? getSunIcon() : getMoonIcon();
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ua-nightmode-choice]').forEach(function (choice) {

  function applyPreference(preference, persist) {

    if (persist) savePreference(normalized);

  }
  function setNightMode(enabled, persist) { applyPreference(enabled ? 'dark' : 'light', persist); }
  function closeMobileFlyout() {
    var flyWrap = document.getElementById('mvp-fly-wrap');

    flyWrap.classList.remove('mvp-fly-menu', 'mvp-fly-shadow');
    document.querySelectorAll('.mvp-fly-but-wrap').forEach(function (button) {
      button.classList.remove('mvp-fly-open');
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.mvp-fly-fade').forEach(function (fade) {
      fade.classList.remove('mvp-fly-fade-trans');
    });
  }
  function buildToggle(options) {
    options = options || {};
    var button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = options.header ? 'ua-nightmode-toggle ua-nightmode-toggle--header' : 'ua-nightmode-toggle';
    button.setAttribute('data-ua-nightmode-toggle', '');
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    button.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Switch to dark mode');
    button.innerHTML = '<span class="ua-nightmode-toggle__icon" aria-hidden="true">' + getMoonIcon() + '</span><span class="ua-nightmode-toggle__label">Night</span>';
    button.addEventListener('click', function () { setNightMode(!root.classList.contains('ua-nightmode'), true); });
    return button;
  }
  function findHeaderToggleSlot() {
    var selectors = [
      '#mvp-main-nav-wrap .mvp-nav-bot-right',
      '#mvp-main-nav-wrap .mvp-nav-top-right',
      '#mvp-main-nav-wrap #mvp-nav-small-right',
      '#mvp-main-nav-small #mvp-nav-small-right'
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < selectors.length; i++) {
      var host = document.querySelector(selectors[i]);

      var search = host.querySelector('.mvp-nav-search-but');
      if (search) return { host: host, search: search };
    }
    var fallbackSearch = document.querySelector('#mvp-main-nav-wrap .mvp-nav-bot-right .mvp-nav-search-but, #mvp-main-nav-wrap .mvp-nav-top-right .mvp-nav-search-but, #mvp-main-nav-small span.mvp-nav-search-but');
    return fallbackSearch && fallbackSearch.parentNode ? { host: fallbackSearch.parentNode, search: fallbackSearch } : null;
  }

  function insertHeaderToggle() {
    var existing = document.querySelector('.ua-nightmode-toggle--header');
    if (mobileQuery.matches) {

  }
  function insertMobileFlyoutControls() {

})();</script><style id="codex-nightmode-flyout-match-mycannabis-late">
