<script id="codex-nightmode-flyout-appearance-desktop">
(function () {
  var root = document.documentElement;
  var storageKey = 'ua_nightmode_preference';
  var valid = { system: true, light: true, dark: true };
  var darkQuery = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)') : { matches: false };
  var labels = {
    en: { appearance: 'Appearance', system: 'System', light: 'Light', dark: 'Dark' },
    fr: { appearance: 'Apparence', system: 'Systeme', light: 'Clair', dark: 'Sombre' },
    es: { appearance: 'Apariencia', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Claro', dark: 'Oscuro' },
    de: { appearance: 'Darstellung', system: 'System', light: 'Hell', dark: 'Dunkel' },
    it: { appearance: 'Aspetto', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Chiaro', dark: 'Scuro' },
    pt: { appearance: 'Aparencia', system: 'Sistema', light: 'Claro', dark: 'Escuro' }
  };

  function normalize(value) { return valid[value] ? value : 'system'; }
  function getPreference() { try { return normalize(localStorage.getItem(storageKey)); } catch (e) { return 'system'; } }
  function savePreference(value) { try { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, normalize(value)); } catch (e) {} }
  function resolvePreference(value) {
    value = normalize(value);
    if (value === 'dark') return true;
    if (value === 'light') return false;
    return !!darkQuery.matches;
  }
  function getLabels() {
    var lang = (root.lang || document.body.getAttribute('lang') || 'en').slice(0, 2).toLowerCase();
    return labels[lang] || labels.en;
  }
  function icon(name) {
    if (name === 'sun') return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"></circle><path d="M12 2v2"></path><path d="M12 20v2"></path><path d="m4.93 4.93 1.41 1.41"></path><path d="m17.66 17.66 1.41 1.41"></path><path d="M2 12h2"></path><path d="M20 12h2"></path><path d="m6.34 17.66-1.41 1.41"></path><path d="m19.07 4.93-1.41 1.41"></path></svg>';
    if (name === 'moon') return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M21 12.8A8.5 8.5 0 1 1 11.2 3a6.8 6.8 0 0 0 9.8 9.8Z"></path></svg>';
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="12" rx="2"></rect><path d="M8 20h8"></path><path d="M12 16v4"></path></svg>';
  }
  function setActive(preference) {
    var normalized = normalize(preference);
    var enabled = resolvePreference(normalized);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ua-nightmode-choice]').forEach(function (choice) {
      var active = choice.getAttribute('data-ua-nightmode-choice') === normalized;
      choice.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
      choice.setAttribute('aria-checked', active ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ua-nightmode-toggle]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.classList.toggle('is-active', enabled);
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
  }
  function applyPreference(preference, persist) {
    var normalized = normalize(preference);
    var enabled = resolvePreference(normalized);
    if (persist) savePreference(normalized);
    root.classList.toggle('ua-nightmode', enabled);
    root.dataset.uaNightmodePreference = normalized;
    root.dataset.uaNightmodeResolved = enabled ? 'dark' : 'light';
    setActive(normalized);
  }
  function buildChoice(value, label, iconName) {
    var button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'ua-nightmode-choice';
    button.setAttribute('role', 'radio');
    button.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
    button.setAttribute('data-ua-nightmode-choice', value);
    button.innerHTML = '<span class="ua-nightmode-choice__icon" aria-hidden="true">' + icon(iconName) + '</span><span>' + label + '</span>';
    button.addEventListener('click', function () { applyPreference(value, true); });
    return button;
  }
  function findMenu() {
    return document.querySelector('#mvp-fly-menu > div > ul.menu, #mvp-fly-menu ul.menu, #mvp-fly-menu-wrap .mvp-fly-nav-menu > div > ul.menu, #mvp-fly-menu-wrap .mvp-fly-nav-menu ul.menu, #mvp-fly-wrap ul.menu');
  }
  function insertControls() {
    var menu = findMenu();
    if (!menu) return null;
    var existing = menu.querySelector('.ua-nightmode-appearance');
    if (existing) {
      setActive(getPreference());
      return existing;
    }
    var text = getLabels();
    var item = document.createElement('li');
    var heading = document.createElement('div');
    var choices = document.createElement('div');
    item.className = 'menu-item ua-nightmode-appearance';
    heading.className = 'ua-nightmode-appearance__label';
    heading.innerHTML = '<span class="ua-nightmode-appearance__icon" aria-hidden="true">' + icon('system') + '</span><span>' + text.appearance + '</span>';
    choices.className = 'ua-nightmode-choice-group';
    choices.setAttribute('role', 'radiogroup');
    choices.setAttribute('aria-label', text.appearance);
    choices.appendChild(buildChoice('system', text.system, 'system'));
    choices.appendChild(buildChoice('light', text.light, 'sun'));
    choices.appendChild(buildChoice('dark', text.dark, 'moon'));
    item.appendChild(heading);
    item.appendChild(choices);
    menu.appendChild(item);
    setActive(getPreference());
    return item;
  }
  function run() {
    insertControls();
    applyPreference(getPreference(), false);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', run, { once: true });
  } else {
    run();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    if (event.target && event.target.closest && event.target.closest('.mvp-fly-but-click, .mvp-fly-but-wrap')) {
      window.setTimeout(run, 60);
    }
  });
  if (darkQuery.addEventListener) {
    darkQuery.addEventListener('change', function () {
      if (getPreference() === 'system') applyPreference('system', false);
    });
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    // 🔧 Remove hidden TOC if present
    const toc = document.querySelector('.table_of_contents_style');
    if (toc) toc.remove();
  });
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  // 🔒 Disable JS handlers on flyout triggers
  document.querySelectorAll('.mvp-search-click').forEach(el => {
    const newEl = el.cloneNode(true);
    el.replaceWith(newEl); // Remove all events
  });

  // 🔒 Remove flyout form container
  const injectedForm = document.querySelector('.mvp-search-form');
  if (injectedForm) injectedForm.remove();
});
</script>
